- **147_scan132 · margin, the first two displayed morphisms** (147_scan132-06): `les $\widetilde\Pi_{\Delta_r}\unsure{\longleftarrow|\longrightarrow}\Pi_{\Delta_r}$ et $\widetilde\Pi\unsure{\text{---}|\longrightarrow}\Pi$`. Only two written morphism groups occur before sont. In the first group the small triangular thickening appears at the left of the shaft; orientation is retained as doubtful. In the second group the shaft has an insufficiently definite terminal tick for resolution. There is no intervening additional Pi-Delta-r-prime to Pi-Delta-r expression.

The preceding live D-star-exclamation group is above and to the right. I remove the unsupported left object and keep the target doubtful.

}`. There are two vertically separated dots and a long upper horizontal stroke with a curved left end. At the bottom of the left vertical, the stem bends right and returns upward in a continuous exposed curve, matching the geometry of the right vertical’s lower return. At the top, the left stem meets a small rounded loop at the cancellation line; a diagonal segment crosses from lower left to upper right through this area. The crossed text conceals their junction, so an independent arrowhead cannot be securely traced. That upper junction is reproduced by its exposed loop and diagonal, with an in-place doubt marker and no categorical arrowhead. The right vertical’s upper pointed end is exposed. The separate oblique stroke and curved, footed stroke farther right are also drawn directly; whether they form a labelled diagonal or a cancelled glyph remains open. The cancelled phrase stays beside the left vertical, with its letters doubtful. Crop 148_scan002_small_diagram.png, original box [181, 1115, 835, 1373].

### Remaining doubts

| Place | Reading | Alternatives | Confidence | Reason |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| cancelled qualifier | \deleted{\unsure{double}} | double; exact full cancelled word unresolved | low | The cancellation crosses low dou-like loops and tall strokes; not all letters can be independently exposed. No grammatical restoration is used. |
| live prefix adjoining cancellation | \unsure{sub}division | sub is the witness reading, but its separate live strokes remain obscured | low | Only division to the right is fully exposed. The preceding dark strokes merge with the cancelled word, so the prefix retains its own local doubt. |

The touching pair has continuous pale equators and asymmetric red point positions. The left meridian sphere has two explicit left equatorial points and a gapped heavy equator. The central rear equatorial arc is heavy and broken around c/d, not a uniform dotted arc. The upper/lower paired spheres have short connectors and distinct solid/dotted coloured paths. The far-right pencil sphere has layered transverse contours and irregular short marks. Selected tetrahedral edges are heavy; the lower rising internal edge is broken and pale. C omits/smooths several of these strokes. No comparison to an unavailable H drawing file is claimed. Additional inspected crops: drawing_upper, drawing_left, drawing_centre, drawing_lower. The source has no closed lower/left sides. C supplies a closed box; H's editorial note places the vertical side at the left. A real comma stands between b and d on the second row; H's thin-space command supplies no comma. The other pair interiors lack separately exposed commas. The first row's last small letter is changed from C/H's c to preferred e with retained c alternative: an upper closed triangular loop and a continuous low return give the e appearance, while retracing leaves c possible.
